HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B001","root_001444/B003","root_001444/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_12461ecbe3709543dbfa","text":"The later chest-language changed this ayah most. Before 114:5, مَلِك could stay at the level of public sovereignty, supported by م ل ك B003. After “في صدور الناس,” م ل ك B001/B005 became active: kingship also means the cohesion/mainstay that keeps the inner person from being fractured by whisper. 114:4’s خناس further made م ل ك B006/B008 visible as central-course and lead-point: sovereignty counters veering, withdrawal, and oscillation. The surprise is that monarchy here governs not only people as a public body but the interior route by which people become movable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B001","root_001444/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4287b478fff2861d5c99","text":"2. **Sovereignty as cohesion of the inner polity.** Evidence: م ل ك B001, strength and cohesion; م ل ك B005, mainstay of a matter, including the heart as body’s mainstay; ص د ر B004 from 114:5, source from which forms issue; و س و س B001 from 114:5, hidden inward whisper. Structural cue: the later battlefield is inside “صدور الناس,” while this ayah names ملك before that interior is revealed. Assigned role: ملك is the power that makes a self or community hold together against internal whisper-fracture. Abductive move: if the attack is inside the chest, kingship must include control of the principle that keeps the inner structure coherent. Reading change: “King” reads as stabilizing sovereignty over the interior source of action, not merely external rule.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5236b6c24186948375f6","text":"1. **Public sovereignty reaches the covert interior.** م ل ك `B003` gives `مَلِكِ` kingship, dominion, and governance of a population. The title is the middle member of `رَبِّ / مَلِكِ / إِلَٰهِ`, each bound to repeated `ٱلنَّاسِ`, but 114:4-5 reveals that the actual disturbance is hidden and takes place `فِى صُدُورِ ٱلنَّاسِ`. The packet supplies public governance and private location; I infer sovereignty -> jurisdiction over the otherwise unseen interior channel. The title thus becomes operational protection rather than a ceremonial rank.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B006","root_001444/B008","root_004965/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6665cacc9a2edec34789","text":"3. **King as central course and lead point.** Evidence: م ل ك B006, center or main course of road/valley; م ل ك B008, leading forepart in animals; ن و س B002, driving camels; خ ن س B005 from 114:4, a horse veering during its run. Structural cue: the surah moves from command/refuge to a sequence of titles, then to a veering/withdrawing whisperer. Assigned role: ملك marks the correct center-line and leading point for a moving body. Abductive move: a community that can sway or be driven requires a sovereign center-course to resist veering. Reading change: the ayah reads as alignment of direction, not only statement of rank.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_86c161878ec563ddd7ca","text":"3. **The sovereign title marks an inner mainstay.** م ل ك `B005` names the support on which a matter depends and specifically images the heart as the body's mainstay. Its later proximity to `صُدُورِ` (ص د ر `B001`) makes that otherwise dormant branch visible: the attack is aimed at the bodily/inner front whose governing support is already named. The packet gives mainstay and breast; I infer possession of the mainstay -> resistance at the point from which conduct is organized. `مَلِكِ ٱلنَّاسِ` consequently acquires an inward axis alongside its social-political one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B003","root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8801d66885c0f52e3113","text":"1. **Kingship as public governance over the wavering many.** Evidence: م ل ك B003, kingship and dominion; ر ب ب B001 from 114:1, lordship/ownership/authority; ء ل ه B001 from 114:3, worship and the worshipped one; ن و س B001, swaying oscillation. Structural cue: this middle title is framed by رب before it and إله after it, all attached to الناس. Assigned role: ملك supplies the public-political register between caretaking lordship and devotional deity. Abductive move: the sequence moves from possession/care to governance to worship so that the same unstable human field is secured at multiple levels. Reading change: “King of people” reads less as isolated royal epithet and more as the governing layer in a protective triad.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9619c75019640053eb82","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:2:1\",\"root_001444\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hükümdarlık, yalnız kamusal yönetime değil, insanların göğsünde işleyen görünmez etki kanalına da uzanan bir egemenlik olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B001","root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a894914bb02db94a9f16","text":"2. **Kingship supplies cohesion to swaying people.** The dormant م ل ك `B001` image is strength and cohesion, explicitly including what holds a wall or a self together; the fixed `ٱلنَّاسِ` activates ن و س `B001`, oscillating or wavering movement. Later, repeated whispering and خ ن س `B001/B005` supply withdrawal and veering. I infer the approach-withdraw influence -> fragmentation or wavering, while `مَلِكِ` -> the cohesion that keeps the human subject from being pulled with it. The phrase can therefore be read materially as the stabilizing hold over an unstable collective and self.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b8c9a618352507f0eb76","text":"114:5 activated م ل ك `B005` retrospectively: its “heart as mainstay” image became functional once the breast was named as the target.
